## 2026-11-07 — USER ASKED HOW TO TEST 0.0.2~gate0audit2; ONE PARKED-CAR ON→OFF→ON PROCEDURE
- User: “Bản này test ntn”. Give ONE contiguous sequence, not repeated QA182 trials or fabricated readback. `com.duoface.carplay.gate0probe 0.0.2~gate0audit2` is an independent, signed rootless **diagnostic** that collects Apple Dashboard presentation/window roles and separate process JSONL; it does NOT fix Google Maps pane or verify authoritative Settings ON/OFF.
- (1) While **parked**, preserve current DuoFace and rollback reference, install the downloaded `com.duoface.carplay.gate0probe_0.0.2~gate0audit2_iphoneos-arm64.deb` locally via Filza/package manager (not Sileo repo); leave currently installed DuoFace QA182 unchanged. No standalone app icon is expected. Disconnect/reconnect CarPlay and reopen Settings to allow target processes to load injected probe. If no new records, cannot assume injection; may need process restart under controlled conditions.
- (2) Same paired car: Settings → General → CarPlay → selected car → Customize. **ON** = Google Maps included/visible on stock Apple CarPlay Home; capture screenshot/clock, reconnect, open stock native Maps once (if icon exists), then choose Google Maps once from DuoFace in split mode, photograph actual display and whether map/other pane survives >=10 seconds.
- (3) **OFF** = remove/hide just Google Maps from **that car's Customize** list, NOT turn CarPlay off globally or stop the Maps iPhone app; screenshot/time, reconnect, verify stock Maps icon absent, choose Maps **from DuoFace picker only** (do not try launching missing stock icon), photograph and observe for >=10 seconds/touch/second pane. This OFF behavior is a user-defined hard product requirement but still unproven.
- (4) **ON restore** = put Google Maps back in same car's Customize, reconnect, repeat one stock + DuoFace selection, restore original state. Record clock times `ON time / OFF time / ON time` so log records can be correlated. A screenshot/manually noted setting is corroboration only; `stockSettings=UNKNOWN` remains until a real per-car programmatic reader is calibrated.
- (5) Export only once at end, through Filza `/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceMapsGate0-CarPlay.jsonl`, `DuoFaceMapsGate0-Preferences.jsonl` (plus `...-CarPlayApp.jsonl` **only if present**) and the usual freshly generated `DuoFaceAudit.txt`; upload in repo `sideplay/log`, plus photo/time notes if possible. Do not conflate earlier Audit13/14 from Oct30 with this test. The evaluator will inspect `version=2`, `auditMirrorWrite`, `area=gate0` line boundaries, `lastPresentWeakReadback`, CarPlay window role and scenario labels; no claim if missing.
- Post-install intended verification: probe log produced + 5-type ABI/scene lifecycle readback correct is **instrumentation PASS only**; OFF native scene/visible/touch/pane/Home requires separately validated Apple Customize source and hardware. If crash/CarPlay hangs, stop test, uninstall **only** `com.duoface.carplay.gate0probe`, restore existing functioning DuoFace. All testing parked; no auto-deploy, no Sileo publish.

Cần hợp nhất điểm này, không cài hook chồng. Membership/declaration không chứng minh eligibility/launch thắng bộ lọc cài đặt khác. Không gate theo settings; nếu log stock-settings thì đọc chỉ phục vụ observation bằng nguồn xác minh, unknown nếu không đọc được, không bịa on/off.
- **Review record:** DFNativeMapLeasePolicy.h remembered-choice hiện chỉ Version/Slot (0 hoặc1), KHÔNG chứa BundleID/session/request. Lease riêng có BundleID/Session/Slot/Timestamp. Không tái diễn giải choice slot-only cũ thành native Maps request mới. Cần assignment identity bundle+physical slot+session/request/generation, lifetime/expiry và replacement rõ ràng; đây là record trạng thái, không phải cờ bật/tắt mới. GitHub search default branch và search branch nativemap không trả kết quả; KHÔNG kết luận code không tồn tại ở mọi ref. Muốn “port code sẵn” phải pin đúng repo/ref/commit/path và bằng chứng ABI iOS16.7.16. Coordinator hiện đã có handleCommand Attach/Restore, adapter.adoptNativeController/restoreOriginalOwner, auto-reconnect và geometry readback, nên không gọi nó thuần observer.

Phải chứng minh origin ở hệ tọa độ native + visual/physical mapping + pane clipping/z-order + native hit-test đúng ô; full rect đọc lại phải khớp Target của kênh geometry thật, một writer. Touch PASS chỉ từ test xe có bằng chứng; phần mềm ghi UNVERIFIED đến lúc đó, không suy từ event/ACK.

OFF là cổng nghiệm thu bắt buộc; ON vẫn hữu ích làm đối chứng nhưng không thay OFF PASS.
- **Đóng gói:** build/archive/publication như người dùng đề xuất chỉ thành kế hoạch khi yêu cầu triển khai được xác nhận; nếu thiết kế giả thuyết chưa vượt catalog/launch gate thì STOP evidence, không lặng lẽ fallback hoặc tuyên bố B1 PASS. Không thay đổi Sileo ở lượt review.

Ảnh chụp màn hình điện thoại nằm ngang với thanh bên CarPlay và hai vùng nội dung: bên trái danh sách rẽ nền tối (Võ Văn Kiệt/Ngô Quyền/Trần Thị Lý), bên phải trang “Đường đi” nền sáng, có các bước rẽ. Cả hai vùng nhìn thấy đều là danh sách chỉ dẫn, không thấy mặt bản đồ/tiles trong khung ảnh.
- **Điều ảnh chứng minh:** tại thời điểm chụp, hai vùng có nội dung chỉ đường và số liệu hiển thị; không phải hai ô trắng hoàn toàn. Không gán chắc mỗi ô là Maps hay Waze từ màu giao diện. Không suy dữ liệu đang cập nhật, cảm ứng hoạt động, native template hay app scene, scene-owner/process hoặc nguyên nhân thiếu mặt bản đồ từ ảnh tĩnh.
- **Liên hệ bước trước:** Audit 9 đã chứng minh các cú bấm Home trong phiên log đó bị standalone bridge nhận. Ảnh mới chưa có log/time correlation để chứng minh hook đã được bypass. Chưa xác nhận người dùng đã tắt “Mở trực tiếp App từ CarPlay Home”. Q1/Q3 stock vẫn unresolved.
- **Tiếp nối:** đối chiếu lượt thao tác tái hiện ảnh với log có qa160-native-icon-forwarded/accepted và scene-owner, giữ hai trường hợp direct-Home ON/OFF riêng; không yêu cầu bật/tắt thêm cài đặt dựa trên phỏng đoán và không coi UI danh sách là bằng chứng root cause. Bước chờ là log của chính lượt tái hiện, không rebuild chỉ từ ảnh.
- **Thực thi lượt này:** chỉ đọc bàn giao GitHub hiện tại và ghi nhận bằng chứng ảnh; không sửa mã, không build/test/deploy, QA181/Sileo/QA180 H1 giữ nguyên.

Two car photos ~05:45/05:46 show Maps navigation active versus route preview; differing UI state alone does not prove differing scene owner. This supersedes prior incorrect assumption of a missing Home icon.

Waze 2/2 accepted, 0 forwarded. Log lines 14845,14863 and repeating 14959/14977. In DFHomeStandalone144.m lines 300-307, DF144Launch calls DF144BeginSelectedBridge and invokes DF144Original only on !bridged. Therefore user tapping native Home Maps icon is NOT a clean stock-launch control; it is DuoFace-owned standalone. Example 05:43:56 CarPlay icon click -> SpringBoard pending-scene -> manager-owned-live-scene with Google Maps pid=4951, full external window 640x360, content 595x360, lines 14881-14891.
- Q2 measured: DuoFace picker Google Maps at line 25429, activate-request 25434 owner=SBAppViewController, FBScene sceneID:com.google.Maps-default attached at 25442-25444, pane 297.5x360 on external 640x360, scale=0.50. In qa181-b0 trace 249 path=duoface records (Maps 130, Waze 117, BKS 2) across both standalone and split. Distinguish standalone versus split using DFStandalone144/Chrome lifecycle, not path field alone. QA181 path=stock has only one observer-ready record at line 25212 (CarPlay pid 4939) and no per-map native-controller / foreground timeline.
- Snapshot missing: exported DuoFaceCarPlayOwnerSnapshot.log section at line 17754 explicitly says [không đọc được hoặc file chưa tồn tại]. Class inventory DBApplicationSceneViewController in native Home snapshots does not identify a Maps scene. Cannot assert native scene nonexistent; genuine stock Q1 unresolved because icon is intercepted and native owner evidence missing.
- Q3 incomplete: only two BKS SpringBoard observations, line 25630 blanked=1 at utc 1794004480.659 and line 25631 blanked=0 at utc 1794004903.304. Zero underlock-observed; no stock Maps scene correlation. No inferred pause/restore writer or decision.
- Verdict: QA181 source CI, immutable archive, public Sileo PASS and QA180 rollback preserved; B0 device Q2 PARTIAL evidence, Q1 BLOCKED by existing interceptor and missing native owner snapshot, Q3 PARTIAL/UNRESOLVED. This supersedes prior 07/11 incorrect no-icon checkpoint but preserves history. Two UI images are consistent with distinct Home standalone versus DuoFace split paths plus route-preview/active-navigation state; cannot assign a particular image to an owner without correlation.

Q1 stock Maps/Waze scene owner và phần Q3 về quyết định hệ thống quanh **stock Maps/Waze scene** không thể thu bằng kịch bản đã yêu cầu vì không có icon để kích hoạt; `path=stock` tuyệt đối không được suy từ DuoFace hoặc record native khác. ## 2026-11-07 — QA181 B0: clarification on "CarPlay Home app" and stock-grid precondition — DEVICE GATE UNVERIFIED
- **Thông tin mới trực tiếp từ Nguyen:** "Carplay home app có xuất hiện đâu mà mở?" — người dùng không thấy mục/app tên "CarPlay Home" để thực hiện bước test mở Maps/Waze từ CarPlay Home gốc. Chỉ ghi nhận đúng báo cáo; **chưa đủ chứng cứ kết luận native Apple App Grid thực sự mất** hay nguyên nhân vì không có ảnh/log xe.
- **Đối chiếu source đã đọc trên feature/duoface-qa181-b0probe1-20261106:** `sideplay/duoface-native-scene-host/DFHomeStandalone144.m` dòng ~299–315 có hook `DBDashboardHomeViewController iconManager:launchIconForIconView:` và comment "genuine icon grid owns Home"; dòng ~649–653 xác nhận "Native Home's icon grid is the ONLY app selection destination." `DFTouchOwnerProbe.m` dòng ~705–706 phân biệt icon DuoFace và các app CarPlay Home ngoại vi. Do vậy CarPlay Home ở work order QA181 là **giao diện/lưới native của Apple, không phải app có tên CarPlay Home để tìm cài hoặc bấm trong lưới DuoFace**.
- **Sửa hướng dẫn test, KHÔNG sửa mã:** (a) nếu màn hình xe có thể quay về lưới launcher CarPlay gốc qua nút Home/App Grid của hệ thống, mở Google Maps rồi Waze TỪ LƯỚI GỐC mỗi app khoảng 10s và thoát; (b) sau đó thử Google Maps từ lưới DuoFace một lần; (c) thu `DuoFaceDeepTrace.log` marker `qa181-b0` và owner-snapshot; đối chiếu Q1/Q2/Q3 dựa trên log. Nếu xe không hiển thị hoặc không vào được lưới gốc, **DỪNG Q1 và phần Q3 gắn stock; đánh dấu blocked/unobservable** chứ không gán các thao tác DuoFace vào path=stock. Cần xác nhận xe có nút Home/App Grid hệ thống hoặc ảnh màn hình trước khi quyết định có thể thực hiện bước đo stock.
- **Bất biến:** Không thay đổi `DFAppTouch177.h`, `DFH1Wake188.m`, Darwin hidden/shown/query, roster/presenter, QA181 DEB hay Sileo. QA181 `0.0.181~b0probe1` đã publish và kiểm SHA công khai ở checkpoint trước, nhưng **B0 device Q1–Q3 chưa được nghiệm thu**. Không bắt đầu B1/B2.
- **Tiếp nối:** Xác định trạng thái thực tế của native CarPlay launcher trên xe từ thao tác Home hoặc ảnh, không phỏng đoán vì tên "CarPlay Home app". Nếu truy cập native Home được thì chỉ tiến hành phép đo B0; nếu không được, mô tả cổng điều hướng chưa truy cập được và đưa ra hạng mục chẩn đoán read-only riêng trước khi yêu cầu test lại.

**Sáu bước nghiệm thu CarPlay, theo thứ tự ưu tiên người dùng:**

1. **Ưu tiên 1 — logo tự ẩn:** mở DuoFace, không tương tác khoảng 4 giây, logo ở giữa thanh chia mờ gần biến mất; chạm/kéo lại thì logo hiện cho thao tác bố cục.

3. **Danh sách app đúng:** menu App hiển thị đúng các ứng dụng có cài và đã bật trong lựa chọn; không có app giả hoặc lựa chọn không hợp lệ.
4. **Ưu tiên 2 — Google Maps/Waze thật:** trong chế độ hai ô, chọn ô trống và chọn Google Maps/Waze; từng app phải hiển thị nội dung bản đồ sống, cảm ứng được, không chỉ icon hoặc vùng đen.
5. **Safari/GOFA/khác giữ đường cũ:** Safari qua AppBridge vẫn hoạt động; GOFA tiếp tục đường ghép ứng dụng hiện hữu, **không** khẳng định có native GOFA controller riêng trong phiên bản này.
6. **Home/quay lại/chia màn hình:** kéo thanh chia, mở app, về Home, trở lại DuoFace; bố cục được khôi phục, không treo app, bàn phím/cảm ứng còn hoạt động.

**Trạng thái từng ca hiện nay:** 1=CHƯA TEST THIẾT BỊ; 2=CHƯA TEST; 3=CHƯA TEST; 4=CHƯA TEST; 5=CHƯA TEST; 6=CHƯA TEST. Các PASS CI chỉ xác nhận build/static audit, **không** đại diện cho pass trên màn hình CarPlay thực.

**Ranh giới thao tác:** chỉ ghi checkpoint; không sửa code/logic attach, logo, chọn app hay bản đồ; không build lại DEB, không publish Sileo. Với các phân tích kế tiếp về Duonavi, chỉ học kiến trúc/đường ghép như tài liệu SidePlay, giữ mã DuoFace độc lập và không sao chép cơ chế license.

**Bước tiếp:** thu ảnh/video thực từ CarPlay cho ca (1) và (4) trước, kèm mô tả thời gian, chế độ một/hai ô, app, thao tác; sau đó chạy (2),(3),(5),(6) và phân loại PASS/FAIL từng ca. Nếu FAIL, đọc đúng mã nguồn feature trên SHA run trên và log hiện có rồi đưa ra sửa đổi hẹp; chỉ cân nhắc DEB khác theo yêu cầu tiếp theo. Không suy đoán kết quả thiết bị.

## 2026-11-03 — BƯỚC 0 QA180 (đọc code + đối chiếu log, chưa sửa gì; bản đầy đủ: workspace duoface-audit/BUOC0_QA180.md)

- **Maps no-kb-window**: trong 31/77 capture kbClass=none, đường fallback UIKeyboardImpl cũng không chạm được đối tượng phím nào (keyplane 0/31) → lúc trượt, process app không có vật thể bàn phím nào với tới được, không phải lỗi một API liệt kê. Exactly the target of the original's `keyboardScreenReferenceSize`/`fixedKeyboardWidth`. Keyplane/input-mode/hwKB are all normal on our side, so a QA179 keyboard layer can be minimal: reference-size/width correction in the app process only. Waze keyboard still unmeasured (this session was Maps only). Keyboard behaviour itself unchanged, by QA178 design — Nguyen confirms.

## 2026-11-03 — QA178 Q&A (answers only, no code changed): keyboard A1-A5, HUD B1-B4

- **A1**: kbdiag enumerates ONLY `connectedScenes` -> `UIWindowScene.windows` + `UIApplication.windows` (DF154GeneralAppStateRelay.m, df176KeyboardShown: ~215-221), first non-hidden window whose class contains "Keyboard". Two rounds of device logs prove those lists never contain the remote keyboard window in the hosted configuration -> kb=nil -> kbClass=none, keyplane search never runs. Log frames come from the notification userInfo.
- **A2**: `DFHostedLogicalSizeForBundle` = hardcoded formula in `DFHostedMapRenderSize.h` (logical = pane x 2.0, bundle ignored). Not prefs, not a table. Per-bundle override = edit that function in that header (call sites DFSceneHost.m x4 + DFHomeStandalone144.m:435).
- **A3**: the header is NOT digest-frozen. DFSceneHost.m IS frozen: digest `a0f62fa5…` pinned in test_qa148/149/150/151 + test_qa161, normalization in test_qa141. Header content is asserted by test_nativeowner_086, test_omnicar_maps_085, test_qa154, test_scenehost_safety, test_youtube_088/089.
- **A4**: the transform in `layoutScene` applies ONLY to the scene host view (`appController.view`); no code scales any keyboard window (the kb window is a separate UIWindow in the app process). Whether iOS composites that window through the scaled scene presentation is NOT established by code or log — marked "khong chac"; QA178's screen-space kb frame must settle it. The 02/11 photo is consistent with compression but is not mechanism proof.
- **A5**: readback (`scene-frame-readback`, DFSceneHost.m:591) compares the FBScene settings frame (actual) against nativeFrame = the logical frame (target). The commit DOES dispatch `setContentReferenceSize:` with the logical size and writes the settings frame in the same transaction, yet readback stays 414x736 across commits: the app client keeps its own phone-logical frame; the design compensates with reference size + transform ("leave the remote player intact"). Whether the frame write is rejected or overwritten by the client is not provable from code (no post-write re-read) — khong chac at that depth.
- **B1**: Chrome `-stop` order: state/generation resets -> QA177 handover gate -> `hideAllExternalWindowsImmediately` (line 2751; the HUD window is hidden HERE unless stashed by the gate) -> HUD view teardown (gofaSpeedHUD=nil) -> heartbeat generation++ and lease retract (only when no handover) -> token cancels -> window ivars nil -> stopAllHosts -> "native12-chrome-stop".
- **B2**: gate at DFNative12Chrome.m:2737 — `hudLease176Published && speedHUDWindow && !speedHUDWindow.hidden && gofaSpeedHUD`. The 03/11 log records NO gate values (handover lines = 0), so which condition was false is not in evidence; code inference: both the lease flag (1s heartbeat, DFNative12Chrome.m:1372) and the hidden check depend on the speed window being un-hidden, and stop's own comment (line 2724) says chrome is hidden at scene deactivation before this runs.
- **B3**: the back-channel EXISTS since QA177: overlay writes its own record `com.duoface.speedhud.overlay163.plist` via `DF177OverlayPublish` (DFSpeedHUDLease176.h:79-90); Chrome reads it via `DF177OverlayVisibleFresh` in the handover tick. Nothing is missing in the channel — the tick that reads it never runs because the handover never starts.
- **B4**: overlay163 logs (i) NSLog (never collected) and (ii) since QA177, file lines `[DuoFaceSpeed8] qa177-overlay attach/detach reason=...` appended to `/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceDockOwner.log`, which IS in the exporter's fixed list — proven by the 03/11 export containing 10 attach + 10 detach lines.

## 2026-11-03 — QA178 STEP-0 REPORTS (read-only, reported before any build)

- **H1(a) kbClass=none — cause found in code**: `df176KeyboardShown:` (DF154GeneralAppStateRelay.m ~215) enumerates windows ONLY via public, scene-scoped API — `connectedScenes` → `UIWindowScene.windows`, plus `UIApplication.windows` — then takes the first non-hidden window whose class contains "Keyboard". `UIRemoteKeyboardWindow` is in neither list in the hosted configuration, so kb stays nil → kbClass=none, kbFrame/kbLevel='?', and the keyplane search (inside `if(kb)`) never runs. The end frames in the log come from the notification's userInfo, not from any window — which is why we have frames but no class. QA178 fix (per WO): private all-windows enumeration behind respondsToSelector + try/catch.
- **H1(b) logical size — premise corrected**: `DFHostedLogicalSizeForBundle` is NOT a fixed 414x736 table and NOT prefs-driven. It is a hardcoded formula in **`DFHostedMapRenderSize.h`**: logical = round(pane x 2.0) per dimension; the bundle argument is ignored ((void)bundleID). Reconciliation of the log numbers: Maps pane 198.33x342.67 x2 = 397x685 = the readback "target"; "actual 414x736" is the APP-side scene size; the kb end-frame width 414 lives in the app's space. Three different sizes — the WO's caution stands: "keyboard compressed with the scene" remains a hypothesis until QA178's screen-space kb frame + host transform land. The 02/11 photo (Waze full-screen) and the Maps pane numbers are separate cases.
- **H1(b) freeze status — better than feared**: the override point is the header alone. The header is NOT the digest-frozen file (frozen = DFSceneHost.m); DFSceneHost.m imports it (line 13), so changing the header changes behaviour WITHOUT touching the frozen blob or its digest — no deliberate digest re-pin needed. Two caveats: DFHomeStandalone144.m imports and calls the same function (an in-header per-bundle gate also affects the standalone surface for those bundles; the signature carries no caller identity); several contract tests reference the formula (test_qa141, test_omnicar_maps_085, test_nativeowner_086, test_qa154, test_scenehost_safety, youtube 088/089) and the map ones need legitimate amendment if the experiment defaults ON for Maps/Waze. Toggle choice (WO asked Muse to pick the simplest user-operable): a Filza-managed kill FILE in Preferences (create = experiment OFF, delete = ON; read live at layout) — same pattern as the project's existing kill files; no host-app UI work.
- **H2 Step-0 — the blocking gate (code inference, not device proof)**: the handover starts only inside `-[DFNative12Chrome stop]` (DFNative12Chrome.m:2737) under FOUR conditions: `hudLease176Published && speedHUDWindow && !speedHUDWindow.hidden && gofaSpeedHUD`. Two of them depend on the speed window being un-hidden: the lease flag is maintained by a 1s heartbeat (`df176HUDLeaseTick:`) whose own visible-test requires `!speedHUDWindow.hidden` (any hidden tick retracts the lease and clears the flag). But stop's own comment (line 2724) states the Native12 CBWindow "destroys/hides chrome at scene deactivation" — i.e. by the time stop runs, the deactivation sequence has typically already hidden the speed window (and the last heartbeat has retracted the lease), so conditions 1 and 3 are false and the branch is dead; hideAllExternalWindowsImmediately then hides everything at once. Secondary inference: app-level exits may not call stop at all (the handover lives only in stop), so those transitions never had a chance — consistent with overlay detach reason duo-session-pulse cycling in the log. QA178's planned fix matches the WO: unconditional stop-time gate-value logging + Retiring at the earliest session-end signal, before any hide.
- Status: Step-0 reported to Nguyen; implementation NOT started, awaiting his go-ahead.

## 2026-11-03 — QA178 PREP: DuoNavi original vs DuoFace comparison (hosting + keyboard + HUD)

- Nguyen asked for the comparison BEFORE fixing keyboard/HUD in QA178. Doc: `~/workspace/duoface-audit/TONG_HOP_QA178_SOSANH_DUONAVI.md` (delivered as attachment). Evidence: read-only strings of the original deb 1.0.3-12 (3 dylibs + DuoFaceUI), QA177 source, QA176/177 device logs. The repo's DuoNaviObserver files are OUR passive observer — they contain no original-runtime behaviour and were not used as evidence about the original.
- **Architecture root difference**: the original's main dylib (DuoFaceDashboard, 7.5MB) injects DIRECTLY INTO THE HOSTED APPS (YouTube, Netflix, Maps, Waze, Spotify, Chrome, Safari, Twitch, TikTok, VLC, Notes, Phone, Music, Settings…) as well as SpringBoard/CarPlay. Ours keeps the host logic in SpringBoard and gives apps only thin reporting relays.
- **Hosting**: original = portal (`CBBridgedPortalAppView`, "Mirror Screen (Portal)") + **`updateSceneSettingsFrame`** (they rewrite the hosted scene's settings frame toward the target geometry) + per-bundle `renderSizeForBundleID:` + a geometry ledger in FullscreenBridge ("content geometry did not match CarPlay display"). Ours = remote FBScene kept at per-bundle LOGICAL size (414x736) and squashed into the pane by a non-uniform transform (actual != target BY DESIGN).
- **Keyboard**: the original runs a dedicated in-app layer `[DuoFaceKB]` — forces hardware-keyboard detection NO (GSEvent + BKSHIDServices), watches UIKeyboardImpl (delegate/input-mode fallback), corrects keyboardName nil/prefix, rescues nil keyplane + forces keyplaneName "letters", and fixes keyboard width against the target reference size (`fixedKeyboardWidth`, `keyboardWidthForScreen:withOrientation:`, `keyboardScreenReferenceSize`). Ours has NO keyboard layer at all (relays are log-only by contract); the keyboard is built in the 414x736 logical space and compressed by the scene transform (width ≈42% of the car display, bottom row clipped). Three root causes recorded: code lives in the wrong process; the logical+scale geometry contract; no CarPlay keyboard adaptation layer. Keyplane on OUR side remains unmeasured (kbdiag kbClass=none even at DidShow) — not proven faulty, just unprotected.
- **HUD**: the original package has NO multi-presenter speed bubble — the bubble and its 3-presenter/2-process arbitration problem are ours alone; the original's UI lives in the CarPlay/dashboard process, not bound to a host-session lifecycle that hides everything at once [inference from package structure].
- QA178 inputs recorded in the doc: keyboard direction (a) change the geometry contract (scene to real pane size — highest risk, frozen DFSceneHost) vs (b) add an in-app keyboard layer to the relays following the 5 behaviour groups (width must account for the scene scale); HUD: unconditional stop-time HUD-state logging + earlier Retiring signal. Not a work order yet.

## 2026-11-03 — QA176 LOG VERDICTS: recall signal source is dead; keyboard = scene-geometry mismatch; HUD exit handover uninstrumented

- The user uploaded the post-test export to GitHub (main, `sideplay/log/`, commit ec4b2b37): `DuoFaceAudit 2.txt` (exported 2026-11-02 23:45Z, covers the QA176 test session) plus two diskwrites_resource .ips from 2026-11-02 10:44/14:01 — both PRE-date QA176 (the 14:01 CarPlay one is the known logging-volume report), not QA176 crashes. Read in full; verdicts below are from the export's own lines. Full evidence summary for the work-order author: local doc `duoface-audit/TONG_HOP_QA177.md`.
- **Ground truth**: the running build WAS 0.0.176~recallhud1 (1,022 `build=` lines, no other label) — the Sileo update landed; "not installed" is excluded.
- **H1 root cause (verified)**: ZERO `qa120-hosted-touch-hit` and ZERO `media-pane-hit-test-target` lines in the whole export, while in-pane touches provably happened (9 keyboard records from tapping Maps search inside a pane). The qa120 line is unthrottled, so `DFSceneSurface.hitTest` never runs during real use — touches into hosted content are routed at scene level, not through the slot window's UIKit hit-test. Therefore `DFSceneHostDidObserveTouchNotification` never fires and QA176's new subscription (and the QA173-era handler design behind it) connects to a dead source; the window recognizer (b) cannot see scene-routed touches either. QA177 must source "user touched the app" from a channel that is alive in this input path (scene lifecycle/input observers, or app-side reporting via the DF156 mailbox) and prove it in logs before wiring it to noteUserActivity.
- **H2 (partially verifiable)**: ZERO `qa176-hud-owner` lines and ZERO `DuoFaceSpeed8` lines — overlay163 (CarPlay process) logs via NSLog only, which the exporter does not collect, so overlay attach/detach has been invisible in every export so far. DF144 activity in the file is from ~7h before the test. The exit flicker sits in the Chrome→overlay handover, which the QA176 lease did not damp (Chrome hides instantly on stop; the overlay re-attaches on its own 1.5s poll after the session pulse decays). QA177: give overlay163 a file-log line on attach/detach, and add handover damping.
- **H3 RESULTS (the diagnostic worked — 9 records, app = Google Maps)**: `hwKB=0` and `inputMode=vi_VN-Telex@sw=QWERTY;hw=Automatic` → NO phantom hardware keyboard, normal software Telex. Keyboard end frames in app space always dock flush at y=736 with width 414 (heights 175/219/270): the keyboard is correct inside a **414x736 portrait phone-sized scene** (scene readback lines confirm hosted scenes really run at 414x736 internally, e.g. actual {414,736} vs target {397,685}), while the display pane is only 342.67pt tall and 270–397 wide → bottom row (space/enter) clipped, width overflowing — exactly the 2026-11-02 photo symptoms. Verdict: the keyboard fault is the scene internal-size vs presented-size geometry contract, NOT Apple's driving ban and NOT a wrong keyplane. Keyplane itself stayed unmeasured: records were captured at WillShow before the keyboard window was visible (kbClass=none) and QA176's own 2s rate limit swallowed the DidShow records — diagnostic flaw to fix in QA177 (capture on DidShow; do not rate-limit across notification names).

## 2026-11-03 — QA176 PACKAGING DECISION (spec Claude) + MUSE REVIEW: AGREE, WITH 4 AMENDMENTS

- **Decision received (spec-writing Claude, user-relayed 03/11):** QA176 = 3 independent items in separate files — (1) controls-recall rewiring (subscribe DidObserveTouch -> realAppTouched:; observeActivityInWindow: only if the recognizer is passive; keep the 4s window as a named constant; anti-storm: extend the deadline without re-layout when already visible, ~250ms processing cap; show/hide through the single QA175 T3 path); (3) single HUD owner by LEASE PRIORITY, not hard disable — Chrome (SpringBoard) > DF144 (SpringBoard) > DF144 (CarPlayDockOwner) > DFSpeedNativeOverlay163; a lease is published only while that HUD is actually visible; yield = hide, ~2s hysteresis; first step read-only census of which window/screen/level each HUD instance occupies (if two live on different screens they do not stack and the premise must be revised); one log line on owner change; acceptance = exactly 1 visible HUD layer; (4) keyboard DIAGNOSTICS ONLY, no behaviour change — per keyboard appearance in a hosted app, one rate-limited log block: app-process side (via a DF155-class relay, Waze is in its filter) keyboard window class+frame, its UIScreen + bounds, keyplane name, input-mode identifier, hardware-keyboard state, UIKeyboardWillShow/DidShow userInfo frames; SpringBoard side: host surface frame + external display size at that moment; all read-only, respondsToSelector/try-catch, behind its own flag. **QA177:** letterbox, the keyboard FIX, DF144 unconditional Home-launch hooks, layout.v1.plist two writers, remaining log writers.
- **Muse review verdict: agree with the packaging and all three item designs** (lease priority over hard disable is the safer call given no evidence yet of where each HUD renders). Amendments recorded for the work order: (A1) `DFNativeActivityObserver` is ALREADY passive by construction in source (cancelsTouchesInView=NO, delays NO, self-Fails immediately, canPrevent/canBePrevented=NO) — the spec's condition is satisfied from source, so observeActivityInWindow: can be wired in the same round; when extending the deadline without layout, the hide timer's generation must be rescheduled too, not only controlsUntil moved. (A2) Do the static half of the HUD census from source BEFORE the work order is finalised (window/screen/level per presenter as coded), so the lease design is not written blind; the device step then only confirms; the "exactly 1 layer" acceptance is measured at steady state, excluding the ~2s handover; DF144 is one source compiled into TWO binaries — the lease change must cover both. (A3) Keyboard diagnostics: add the keyboard window's owning scene and its frame relative to its own screen bounds to the same log block, so overflow-vs-clipped is decidable from one block. (A4) None of the 3 items needs to touch the frozen `DFSceneHost.m` (the touch notification already exists there; subscription and all lease/diagnostic code live in Chrome/DF144/overlay163/DF155 files) — keep that as a work-order constraint.

## 2026-11-02 — KEYBOARD RESEARCH: NOT THE CARPLAY DRIVING BAN — THE ORIGINAL HAS A KEYBOARD LAYER DUOFACE LACKS

- **User's hypothesis checked (web + platform facts):** stock CarPlay does restrict typing — template apps get a keyboard only when the vehicle is stationary (voice/Siri while moving), and many head units gate keyboard input behind the parking-brake interlock. That mechanism does NOT explain the Waze photo: (1) the photo was taken parked at home, where the ban does not apply, yet the keyboard was still broken; (2) a ban suppresses the keyboard or forces voice — here the keyboard APPEARS, just with wrong layout (narrow, bottom row missing); (3) the photo shows a hosted phone UI, not a CarPlay template, so the template keyboard rules do not govern it directly.
- **Original DuoNavi cross-check (static strings, read-only, local deb `com.duonavi.app_1.0.3-12`):** `DuoFaceDashboard.dylib` contains a dedicated keyboard subsystem tagged `[DuoFaceKB]`. Log strings prove it: forces hardware-keyboard detection OFF (`BKSHIDServicesIsHardwareKeyboardAttached`/`GSEventIsHardwareKeyboardAttached` -> forcing NO — a CarPlay connection otherwise makes iOS treat the car as an attached hardware keyboard), corrects nil/prefixed keyboard and keyplane names in `UIKeyboardLayoutStar setKeyboardName:`/`setKeyplaneName:` (the keyplane name selects the layout — a wrong/nil keyplane is the prime suspect for the missing space/return row), forces a fallback input mode in `UIKeyboardImpl` when lookup fails, logs `UIRemoteKeyboardWindow` frame/hidden/alpha, and fixes keyboard width for the car screen (`fixedKeyboardWidth`, `keyboardWidthForScreen:withOrientation:`, `keyboardScreenReferenceSize`, `setKeyboardWidth:`). The original authors hit exactly this failure class and handled it.
- **SidePlay cross-check (pre-extracted strings of SidePlay 1.0.4-1 in `sideplay/audit/deb-1.0.4-1/macho-strings`):** only the four standard `UIKeyboard*Show/HideNotification` references — it observes keyboard appearance; no keyplane/input-mode/width repair. **DuoDash/OmniCar:** not verified at string level in this pass (the project's six-deb audit indexed BKS/display references, not keyboard strings); project records describe DuoDash as a repack of the original DuoNavi, so it likely inherits the same KB layer — marked UNVERIFIED.
- **Conclusion / updated QA176 keyboard item:** the fault is not Apple's driving ban; DuoFace is missing the keyboard layer entirely. Scope (behavioural, clean-room): (1) force software-keyboard mode against the car's phantom hardware-keyboard signals; (2) ensure the keyplane/input mode resolved for the external display is valid (prime suspect for the lost bottom row); (3) fix keyboard width to the hosted frame. Diagnose first with the same observations the original logs (frame + keyplane after showKeyboard), then fix behind a kill switch.

## 2026-11-02 — CORRECTION 2: KEYBOARD FAULT IS WIDTH + MISSING BOTTOM ROW (not just size)

- The user pointed out what the earlier photo analysis missed: besides being small/compressed, the hosted Waze keyboard has **no bottom row at all — no spacebar and no return/enter key**. The letter rows (QWERTY / ASDF / shift-ZXCV-delete) and the suggestion strip render; the function row that completes the keyboard is missing/clipped below the visible area. The keyboard is therefore not merely small but unusable: spaces cannot be typed and input cannot be confirmed.
- This corrects the "full QWERTY rows are present" phrasing in the device-photo entry below: the letter rows are present, the bottom row is not. Refined fault statement for the QA176 keyboard item: (1) keyboard width compressed to ~45% (phone-width frame), (2) bottom row (space/enter) cut off — the keyboard's frame/anchoring does not fit the hosted area vertically either. Both point to the same root: the keyboard window keeps phone geometry inside a scene hosted at external-display geometry, and no DuoFace machinery manages it (relays skip "Keyboard" windows). The diagnose-first step must log the keyboard window's frame AND its host/clipping bounds to separate "frame extends past the bottom edge" from "clipped by an ancestor".

Muse misidentified it from the photo; the entry below is corrected on this point (including the mic-button fallback, which is Waze's search mic).
- Everything else in that entry stands unchanged: the app UI spans the full CarPlay width while its keyboard is compressed into the left ~45% at the bottom; no DuoFace geometry machinery manages keyboard windows, and the relays explicitly skip "Keyboard" windows. The QA176 diagnose-first keyboard plan is unaffected. Note for the record: Waze here is rendered through DuoFace scene hosting (full phone UI), not through its native CarPlay template UI.

## 2026-11-02 — DEVICE PHOTO: HOSTED-APP KEYBOARD RENDERS WITH WRONG GEOMETRY (plan: QA176 keyboard item, diagnose first)

- User photo (22:45 VN, QA175 on device, GOFA hosted full-screen on the car display, search field focused): the app itself fills the full CarPlay width correctly (search bar and category chips span the screen), and the keyboard DOES appear — correcting the earlier same-evening statement that nothing shows: the full QWERTY rows are present but compressed into roughly the left ~45% of the screen width along the bottom, suggestion strip equally narrow, blank space to its right; keys are squeezed too small to type comfortably.
- Diagnosis (photo-verified + source-verified): the keyboard rides along with the hosted app scene, but no DuoFace geometry machinery manages the keyboard window — and the window relays explicitly skip windows whose class contains "Keyboard" (DFAppViewportRelay.m:68, DF154GeneralAppStateRelay.m:146, DFYouTubeOrientationRelay.m:220), so the keyboard keeps a phone-width frame anchored bottom-left while the app scene is stretched to the full external width. - Agreed approach to propose for QA176 (diagnose first, per project habit): step 1 — log the keyboard window's class/frame/scene when it presents inside a hosted app, no behaviour change; step 2 — only then force the keyboard frame to the hosted pane's full width anchored to its bottom (and follow pane resize/swap), behind a kill-switch flag. Fallbacks noted honestly: CarPlay itself restricts typing while driving, and GOFA's search has a mic button (visible in the photo) — voice input and phone-side typing remain the interim workarounds. No code changed in this discussion.

## 2026-11-02 — INVESTIGATION: CONTROLS RECALL / NON-CARPLAY APPS / HUD LAYERS / KEYBOARD (source-verified, no code changed)

- **Controls recall (logo + ellipsis) — mechanism exists, wiring cut (proven from QA175 source).** Design intent: `DFSceneSurface` hitTest posts `DFSceneHostDidObserveTouchNotification` (`com.duoface.scene.actual.touch`, object = surface) on real pane touches (DFSceneHost.m:704); Chrome's `realAppTouched:` (DFNative12Chrome.m:1280) would answer with `noteUserActivity` → `controlsUntil = now + 4s`, showing per-pane pills (ellipsis menu + zoom) and the divider logo at full alpha (idle alpha is 0.02, logo alpha set at :1632). BUT Chrome registers observers ONLY for `DFSceneHostDidBecomeReadyNotification`/`DFSceneHostDidFailNotification` (:697-700) — the touch notification is never subscribed, so `realAppTouched:` is dead code; the Chrome-window touch-listener installer `observeActivityInWindow:` (:642) likewise has zero call sites. Net live recall paths: tapping Chrome's own still-visible controls, divider interaction, or the near-invisible logo itself (wake-only first tap, :2289-2309) — matching the user's device report that only an exact logo tap brings the buttons back, unlike the old behaviour where any touch recalled them. **QA176 candidate fix (small, restores the original design): subscribe Chrome to DidObserveTouch and call observeActivityInWindow: for Chrome's own windows.**
- **Non-CarPlay apps on the CarPlay screen — route is scene hosting, not entitlement spoofing (verified).** Eligibility (`DFAppEligibility.h`): every third-party app is eligible; Apple apps only via a user-facing whitelist; system services, CarPlay Home and DuoFace's own bundles excluded. `DFSceneHost` hosts the app's remote scene into slot windows on the external display; the entitlement route (`DFFirstPartyAdmission`) stays dormant behind its double gate and is not the shipping path. Verdict: mechanism is the intended one and works (e.g. YouTube panes), with two open defects attached: the letterbox dead region and the broken controls-recall chain above.
- **Speed HUD multiple layers — NOT fixed.** Audit finding A1 stands: `DFNative8SpeedHUDView` is presented from 4 places (Chrome `gofaSpeedHUD` + DF144 `speedHUD`, both SpringBoard; DF144 compiled into DFCarPlayDockOwner + `DFSpeedNativeOverlay163` singleton, both in the CarPlay process), all reading the same prefs/GPS feed, so instances stack; the only suppression is overlay163 yielding to a fresh (<2s) DF144 lease. QA173/174/175 did not touch it; a single presentation owner remains QA176 scope.
- **Keyboard in hosted (non-CarPlay) apps — NOT handled.** The only keyboard references in the whole source are exclusions: the app-state/viewport relays skip windows whose class name contains "Keyboard" (DF154GeneralAppStateRelay.m:146, DFAppViewportRelay.m:68, DFYouTubeOrientationRelay.m:220). Nothing presents or forwards a hosted app's keyboard to the CarPlay display; text input stays in the app's own process (iPhone side).

## 2026-11-02 — QA172 GOFA PAYLOAD DECODER STARTED (G0 STATIC FIELD MAP DONE); NO CODE, NO BUILD, NO DEVICE TEST

- **User decision recorded:** proceed with Method 2 — DuoFace reads GOFA data itself with a clean-room decoder following the original's proven mechanism. Method 1 (bundling/side-loading the original `SpeedAlerts.dylib`) is rejected and closed: it redistributes a commercial binary with its own activation, keeps all data locked inside the vendor collector (no export path exists), and would only double-render overlays. No vendor code, binary, asset or license material is incorporated into DuoFace; only interface facts are used.
- **Gate G0 completed this session (Muse, static read-only):** the original payload helpers were traced at instruction level against the real Mach-O bytes (DEB SHA256 `e5b0918b…`): `__objc_selrefs`, `__cstring`, `__cfstring` and Swift small-string immediates resolved to literals — superseding the earlier first-65-instructions samples. Full evidence: `sideplay/audit/qa172-gofa-payload-decoder-20261102/FIELD_MAP.md`; design and gated plan: `DESIGN.md`; session status: `STATUS.md` in the same folder.
- **New verified facts (instruction-level):** speed board lives in `userInfo["pushSpeedBoard"]` (NOT `note.object`); `gofaSpeedPayload` (`0x3e390`) checks `note.name == notificationSpeedBoard`, bridges `userInfo`, converts the value with the shared Mirror helper `0x3da6c`, checks the dynamic type name `GOFA.SpeedBoardModel`, and emits a new dictionary with the found subset of `userSpeed`, `speed`, `currentAllowSpeed`, `limit`; the speed callback stores it via `setGofaBoard:` and reads `board["limit"]`. Alert payload keys differ by board: `pushNextBoard` vs `pushOtherBoardToCarplay`; the value is `Swift.Array<GOFA.DatapointModel>`; per point the original reads `traffic_sign_id`, `lat`, `lon` and normalises to `{identity, sign, latitude, longitude}` (collector reads back exactly those keys). A separate Flutter method-channel mechanism (`flutterDirection`, `decodeMethodCall:`, ~`0x3c1e8`) exists but is NOT traced — maneuvers stay out of QA172 v1 scope.
- **DuoFace-side readiness verified in current source:** the GOFA dylib filter is exactly `com.lumi.GOFA` and already compiles the probe that receives these notifications; `DFSpeed::Sample`/protocol already carry and validate a limit (<=200, freshness 5s) and the HUD already renders a limit ring — the only blocker is the deliberate `hasLimit` rejection in `DFSpeedTransport.mm`'s GPS publish path. Plan: Swift Mirror-based decoder (first-party, bounded, structurally validated, type names logged never hard-required) inside the GOFA process only; shadow mode before any HUD publishing; no new swizzling; no synthetic fallback values.
- **Gates:** G0 static map DONE → G1 runtime shape evidence from the QA171 device log (notifications fire? `push*` keys present? value classes?) — no decoder code ships before G1 → G2 decoder core + host tests with synthetic models → G3 shadow integration, private build, device comparison against GOFA's own UI (also settles `userSpeed` vs `speed` semantics, still unknown) → G4 transport + HUD → G5 release only with separate authorisation and the installed-package conflict inventory. QA170 remains the public rollback.
- **Current execution status:** documentation/design only. No DuoFace source changed, no build, no Sileo publication, no device test. Flags remain `DEVICE_TESTED=false`, `SILEO_PUBLISHED=false`, `GOFA_REAL_ROAD_ALERTS_CONNECTED=false`, `GOFA_REAL_NAVIGATION_TURNS_CONNECTED=false`.

- User physical screenshot of CarPlay Home (phone photo): external Home shows oversized black/red X (not polished); reports NO X inside DuoFace when dragging bubble and that dismiss does not persist across host transitions. User asks for a concrete UI/behavior proposal. **No change to source or new DEB during this explanation checkpoint; this is verified source diagnosis and proposed fix, not implemented.**
- Reviewed CURRENT QA168 `sideplay/duoface-native-scene-host/speed8/DFNative8SpeedHUDView.mm`: `showDismissTarget` returns immediately when `floatingWindowMode` is true (line 257); `pan:` explicitly rejects dismiss for floating mode `!self.floatingWindowMode` (line 291); external X explicitly 62x62 black opaque circle, red border, font 38 and 18pt extra proximity allowance (lines 258-270, 290). Within DuoFace `DFNative12Chrome.m` makes `gofaSpeedHUD.floatingWindowMode=YES` on a bubble-sized dedicated external window (lines 1038-1051), therefore X cannot appear / dismiss inside DuoFace **by existing source design, not a hypothetical symptom**.
- Current `dismissedForSession=YES` is only a property on one `DFNative8SpeedHUDView` and does not persist. `DFSpeedNativeOverlay163.m -poll:` re-creates a NEW view when selected CarPlay window changes (lines 123-140), which resets state; `DFNative12Chrome.m -refreshGlobalSpeedHUD164` controls window.hidden only from connection/startup, never a shared user-disabled state (lines 1601-1615). These are source-supported reasons the X drag hide can reappear after a host re-create; exact reported device event sequence remains unproven without logs.
- **PROPOSED design, pending implementation:** one first-party shared state owner in the existing `com.duoface.speedhud` preferences + app-scoped Darwin change notification: `enabled` persistent across CarPlay/host changes; `autoHidden` per active session; `dragging` transient; only deliberate Enable action from DuoFace iPhone app control or existing DuoFace CarPlay menu re-enables after genuine dismiss. No background poll or new window may override disabled. Explicitly distinguish temporarily hidden (restorable without forced touches on all apps) vs disabled by dragging to X (sticky until enable). All three owners `DFNative12Chrome`, `DFSpeedNativeOverlay163`, and standalone Home attach must honor same policy; iOS mobile control must update shared state so bubble can be turned ON from outside DuoFace.
- **PROPOSED X UX:** show ONLY during bubble drag. Soft semi-transparent 42-46pt circular target with discreet xmark (no rectangular black plate and harsh red ring), bottom-center safe zone; expand hit region invisibly, animate highlight at drag-over and dismissal fade; remove target immediately on drag end/cancel. In DuoFace, bubble-sized floating UIWindow cannot show bottom X outside bounds: create a minimal visual drop-target owned by existing external Chrome/scene with no full-screen hit interceptor, calculate drop position in common CarPlay screen coordinates. External CarPlay owner uses same target style/hit geometry. Do not break Dock, app touch, Home or split drag. Re-enable through existing iPhone app controls / DuoFace menu, no ambiguous auto-show.

## 2026-11-01 — VERIFIED MECHANISM/FEATURE COMPARISON: QA165 DuoFace Speed8 vs CarPlayPro Speed 0.9.97
- **Method/source:** direct comparison of `feature/duoface-qa165-gofa-samples-home-roster-20261101:sideplay/duoface-native-scene-host/{DFGofaNativeLocationTap165.m,DFGofaSpeedSource162.m,DFSpeedNativeOverlay163.mm,build.sh,speed8/DFSpeedTransport.mm,speed8/DFSpeedProtocol.hpp,speed8/DFNative8SpeedHUDView.mm}` vs original CarPlayPro 0.9.97 **static** evidence on `Duoface/main:sideplay/audit/carplaypro-0.9.97/TECHNICAL_FEATURE_MAP.md`, original `com.carplaypro.speed_0.9.97_iphoneos-arm64.deb` SHA256 `e5b0918b3bc1948420f37a8a9e21365756da46fba44d582a3f2893aee9b4102f`, audit CI run `41292790813`. Original vendor process selector symbols/plists are **not original ObjC source** or runtime confirmation.
- **Similarity proven only at design level:** both involve injection into GOFA process and CarPlay-related processes, a speed/overlay concept, CoreLocation-related selector presence in original; **not** proof that both use the same GPS producer, IPC, GOFA field mapping or UI-window algorithm.
- **DuoFace actual QA165 producer:** compiled `DFGofaSpeedSource162.m` **with `-DDF165_PASSIVE_GOFA_ONLY=1`**, so its old `[CLLocationManager new]` autonomous startup is **disabled**. Added `DFGofaNativeLocationTap165.m` intercepts `CLLocationManager.setDelegate:` and delegate `locationManager:didUpdateLocations:` in `com.lumi.GOFA` only and forwards original callback first. If callback occurs and GPS age -2..8 s, horizontal accuracy 0..100 m, speed 0..111.12 m/s are valid, converts `round(mps*3.6)` and publishes. **Actual GOFA callback and data arrival NOT device-verified**; unknown if GOFA even uses this path at the needed time. No proprietary GOFA speed-limit/camera/turn model.
- **DuoFace actual processing:** custom 64-bit `DFSpeedProtocol.hpp` message over Darwin notify `com.duoface.speedhud.v1.gofa`, 5-second TTL; `DFSpeedTransport.mm` only accepts GOFA source, `hasCurrent=YES,hasLimit=NO,limitKmh=0`. Although enum lists Google Maps, Waze and VietMap, **there are no connected publishers for those sources** in this transport. HUD `DFNative8SpeedHUDView.mm` renders own single pill, draggable, long-press settings (scale 0.8–1.35, opacity 0.5–1, show/hide *empty* speed-limit ring); value becomes `—` when no fresh sample. No real speed limit, camera warning, navigation turn/distance or overspeed logic. Settings labels including “Nguồn: GOFA” are NOT evidence that other source adapters work.
- **DuoFace actual display paths:** `DFNative12Chrome.m` QA164 small independent external UIWindow spans DuoFace full contentRect; `DFHomeStandalone144.m` QA165 extra tiny HUD UIWindow over standalone SpringBoard-hosted apps (e.g. Safari); `DFSpeedNativeOverlay163.mm` finds visible external CarPlayApp/CarPlay/Dashboard UIWindow and attaches HUD view, suppressing duplicates via leases. Compilation/publish PASS; physical z-order/touch/general app support **UNVERIFIED**. QA163 physical feedback was HUD confined to one pane and speed missing.

Unlike CarPlayPro, DuoFace QA165 does not implement these data features; GOFA speed is only tentative.

CarPlayPro original not runtime tested by us; user reported its HUD does not show on DuoFace, which static findings cannot override. DuoFace QA165 has successful signed CI+Sileo publication, NOT proof of real GPS, multi-app overlay or alert correctness. Do not call QA165 “based on CarPlayPro GOFA data source” or “feature equivalent”. ## 2026-11-01 — SOURCE PROVENANCE CORRECTION: QA165 GOFA speed is NOT the proven CarPlayPro Speed data pipeline
- **User question:** Whether GOFA speed data was adapted from original CarPlayPro Speed or independently invented. **Verified answer: independently engineered CoreLocation-only candidate; NOT a reimplementation of CarPlayPro's verified GOFA-specific data contract. - **QA162–QA164 actual source:** `sideplay/duoface-native-scene-host/DFGofaSpeedSource162.m` constructs `[CLLocationManager new]` within GOFA process, accepts valid iOS `CLLocation.speed` and calculates `round(speed*3.6)`. It does not read GOFA's app-specific model, speed-limit, camera or navigation feed. Published with custom `DFSpeedTransport.mm` Darwin notify `com.duoface.speedhud.v1.gofa` and 5-second `DFSpeedProtocol.hpp` TTL; `limitValid=false`.
- **QA165 actual change:** `sideplay/duoface-native-scene-host/DFGofaNativeLocationTap165.m` hooks GOFA-process `CLLocationManager.setDelegate:` and concrete `locationManager:didUpdateLocations:` delegates, forwarding original callback before publishing speed. Prior independent GPS-manager constructor is disabled via `DF165_PASSIVE_GOFA_ONLY`. This only attempts to observe CoreLocation readings GOFA already receives, and has **no measured in-device proof that such a callback runs or carries usable data**. It does not obtain proprietary GOFA alerts/road limits/cameras.
- **CarPlayPro original evidence:** `main:sideplay/audit/com.carplaypro.speed_0.9.97_iphoneos-arm64.deb` original SHA256 `e5b0918b3bc1948420f37a8a9e21365756da46fba44d582a3f2893aee9b4102f`; static audit `main:sideplay/audit/carplaypro-0.9.97/TECHNICAL_FEATURE_MAP.md`, workflow 41292790813. `SpeedAlerts.dylib` filter includes GOFA, CarPlayApp and other source apps, with `SACollector`, `gofaAlertGroups`, `cameraUpcoming`, and CoreLocation-related selectors. **Symbols/strings alone do not reveal proprietary GOFA speed/limit/camera field contract or runtime path.** No such original contract has been independently validated or ported to DuoFace.
- **Acceptance gate:** QA165 signed DEB + public Sileo success is not a real GOFA data integration result. Keep status **UNVERIFIED SOURCE, SPEED LIMIT/CAMERA NOT IMPLEMENTED** until actual GOFA → callback → IPC → HUD on-device syslog and visual measurement. If no callback, audit specific GOFA/CarPlayPro data owner from evidence; do not invent fields or report numbers. Exporter `DFNativeDiagnostics.m` does not yet capture GOFA probe NSLog or `DuoFaceHomeRoster165.log`. Do not disguise these missing logs as evidence.

## 2026-11-01 — QA164: user-reported no GPS sample + split-pane-only Speed8 drag
- **Confirmed user feedback from installed QA163:** Speed8 displays without real speed data; within DuoFace it is confined to **one split pane** and cannot drag over the full DuoFace layout. This feedback is *not* evidence that native CarPlay Dashboard overlay works. Do not label QA163 a device PASS.
- **Exact root cause for ONE-PANE drag (source proved):** QA163 `DFNative12Chrome.m -refreshGlobalSpeedHUD163` attached `DFNative8SpeedHUDView` as `[ownerWindow.rootViewController.view addSubview:]` where `ownerWindow` was one `appSceneWindows[i]`. HUD `DFAllowedArea` clamps center using `self.superview.bounds`; hence any pan cannot cross into another physical split UIWindow. This was a **mount/coordinate-system design bug**, not a gesture timer. Existing source showed `makeWindow:` can create independent bubble-sized, own-contentRect external UIKit windows without changing hosted scenes/Dock.
- **Why GPS data is NOT available:** QA162/163 never read GOFA app's proprietary speed/road information. `DFGofaSpeedSource162.m` creates a **new CoreLocationManager inside GOFA process**, requires actual GOFA authorization and OS updates, accepts only speed>=0 and horizontalAccuracy<=100m / age<=8s; publishes to `DFSpeedTransport.mm` Darwin notify only while GOFA process executes. `DFSpeedProtocol.hpp` rejects samples after **5 seconds**; changing CarPlay app or app suspension can leave `—`. `limitValid=false`, no real road limit or camera. **No real device authorization/sample log yet**, so cannot assert which of these runtime conditions occurred. Logging-only work adds `GOFA GPS source status auth=`, `GOFA GPS sample REJECTED`, `GOFA GPS IPC publication FAILED`, and HUD `viewer NO_FRESH_SAMPLE source=GOFA ttl=5s`; no GPS coordinates logged. The underlying GOFA proprietary adapter remains **NOT CONNECTED**.

- **Actual code provenance:** `sideplay/duoface-native-scene-host/speed8/DFNative8SpeedHUDView.mm` và `DFSpeedProtocol.hpp` là phần HUD/protocol DuoFace **đã có trước QA162**. Mới tự viết `DFGofaSpeedSource162.m` và `speed8/DFSpeedTransport.mm`: module được inject vào process bundle `com.lumi.GOFA` qua `DFGofaSpeedSource162.plist`, **tạo CLLocationManager mới** trong process GOFA, đọc `CLLocation.speed`, đổi m/s→km/h `location.speed * 3.6`, phát bởi Darwin notify `com.duoface.speedhud.v1.gofa`, HUD đọc và hiển thị. Dữ liệu đến từ **CoreLocation của iOS**, **không phải từ bộ phát tốc độ GOFA nội bộ**. Việc có quyền/location và chạy được khi host/screen off CHƯA ĐƯỢC XÁC MINH. `hasLimit=false`, `limitKmh=0`, **không có cảnh báo camera/giới hạn tốc độ/điều hướng GOFA**.
- **Correction to prior status:** Câu “đã nối nguồn GOFA” chỉ đúng theo nghĩa **inject một CoreLocation reader riêng vào tiến trình GOFA**; không đúng theo nghĩa *đã nối nguồn dữ liệu thật do GOFA cung cấp*. ## 2026-11-02 — Root cause: Speed8 shows CoreLocation GPS, not GOFA traffic/navigation feed

- **User report:** Speed8 displays only the phone/GPS speed and does not show the expected GOFA-source data.
- **Confirmed source-level cause (not a device diagnosis):** QA170 does not yet integrate GOFA's proprietary/Flutter traffic and navigation data model. Its current numeric speed path is CoreLocation only:
  - `DFGofaNativeLocationTap165.m` passively wraps `CLLocationManager.setDelegate:` inside bundle `com.lumi.GOFA`, forwards GOFA's original delegate callback first, and publishes `CLLocation.speed * 3.6` only if the callback actually occurs and its timestamp/accuracy/speed pass validation. Whether GOFA uses this exact callback path on the user's device is **not verified**.
  - `DFGofaSpeedSource162.m` is a first-party fallback `CLLocationManager` running inside the GOFA process. It starts only after 10 seconds without a fresh native publish and only if GOFA already has Location authorization; it does not request permission. This is still an iOS GPS reading, not a read of GOFA's internal traffic model.
  - `speed8/DFSpeedTransport.mm` accepts only GOFA-tagged current speed, explicitly requires `hasLimit=false` and `limitKmh=0`; the protocol freshness window is 5 seconds. The enum lists Google Maps/Waze/VietMap, but there are no connected publishers for those providers.
- **Why GOFA alerts/limits/turns are absent:** `DFGofaTrafficNavProbe166.m` observes candidate notification names `notificationNextBoard`, `notificationOtherBoard`, `notificationSpeedBoard`, but the current implementation exports only event index, object category, key count, safe class/property/key/type metadata. It does not decode or publish actual alert/limit/route values. The source comment explicitly says actual GOFA posting remains an on-device hypothesis. `DF167GOFAEventProtocol.h` carries metadata only, no route/GPS/alert values.
- **Navigation adapter is only conditional:** `DF168PublicCarPlayManeuverAdapter.m` can forward a real public Apple `CPManeuver` plus `CPTravelEstimates` if the observed notification object actually contains one. No evidence proves GOFA emits that public object; the real GOFA Swift `DatapointModel/SpeedBoardModel` mapping is still unknown/unconnected.
- **CarPlayPro comparison remains static-only:** original DEB audit identifies GOFA-related `gofaAlertGroups`, `cameraUpcoming`, CoreLocation and collector/overlay symbols in `SpeedAlerts.dylib`; it does not reveal a validated runtime GOFA payload contract or prove how those values are read. Do not copy vendor code/license or invent field names.
- **Status:** this explains the missing GOFA data from the checked-in source; it does not prove whether the device currently receives the passive callback or fallback GPS sample. No code was changed and no new build/device test was run in this checkpoint. QA170 public package status remains as recorded above; real road alerts, camera/speed-limit data and GOFA turn guidance are **NOT CONNECTED / NOT VERIFIED**.
- **Next evidence-driven step:** while parked, run GOFA with its existing Location permission, capture the exact `DuoFaceSpeed8` lines (native delegate observed/callback, fallback start, valid publish, no fresh sample, IPC failure) and the GOFA notification diagnostic/syslog. First establish whether the three original notification names are actually posted and capture their real runtime object schema safely; only then implement a first-party field adapter and connect real values to the cross-pane HUD. Keep values unknown until the real feed is proven.
- **Skill procedure note:** read `Gpt-training-/SKILL_INDEX.md`, `PROJECT_INSTRUCTIONS.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and `DEB_IPA_SKILL.md`. The matching `skills/02-ios-deb-ipa-carplay.zip` was fetched, but binary extraction did not complete in this session; no claim that ZIP contents were read. No project code/build was changed.

## 2026-11-02 — Correction: CarPlayPro GOFA pipeline already statically mapped; QA171 is diagnostics, not the implementation (2026-11-02)

- **Correction to the simplified QA170 status above:** the QA166 original-DEB reverse engineering already proved the main CarPlayPro GOFA event route. It is incorrect to say that the static audit did not reveal how CarPlayPro reads GOFA at all. The exact notification registration and callback path are in `sideplay/audit/carplaypro-0.9.97/QA166_VERIFIED_GOFA_NOTIFICATION_ROUTE_20261101.md` and `qa166-gofa-dataflow/GOFA_CALL_GRAPH.md`.
- **Original binary facts (static, exact original SpeedAlerts.dylib):** setup function `0x3ce1c` calls `NSNotificationCenter.defaultCenter` and the verified selector `addObserverForName:object:queue:usingBlock:`. Its static NSArray contains `notificationNextBoard` and `notificationOtherBoard`; a separate CFString registers `notificationSpeedBoard`. The original blocks resolve to alert callback `0x3cfb4` and speed callback `0x3d468`. Alert callback calls `_SAGofaAlertPayload` at `0x3e93c` / Swift helper `0x3e940`; speed callback calls `_SAGofaSpeedPayload` at `0x3e38c` / Swift helper `0x3e390`.
- **Original state/render structure (static):** `SACollector` owns typed state including `gofaBoard`, `gofaAlertGroups`, `gofaPassedPoints`, `gofaAlertLocation`, `gofaBoardAt`, `alertsUpcoming`, and `cameraUpcoming`; `SAOverlay` is a separate rendering owner. This gives the architecture to reproduce independently: GOFA event observer → typed payload extraction → first-party collector/state → HUD renderer. No CarPlayPro code, binary, activation or license is to be copied into DuoFace.
- **Why QA170/QA171 still show only GPS:** DuoFace's current Speed8 path remains CoreLocation speed, and `DFGofaTrafficNavProbe166.m`/QA171 only observes the proven notification names and records sanitized metadata/counts. It does not decode the Swift payload, populate road-alert/turn samples, or connect those values to the HUD. QA171 is a diagnostic aid, not the actual CarPlayPro-style implementation.
- **Remaining unknowns (do not guess):** the exact Swift runtime payload object/ABI and mapping of `GOFA.DatapointModel` / `GOFA.SpeedBoardModel` fields into allowed speed, alert category/icon, camera/sign type and ahead distance, maneuver direction and remaining distance; also whether these notifications fire in the user's live GOFA session. Static strings and notification names alone do not prove those field values or device delivery.
- **Correct next implementation path:** continue static analysis of the complete original Swift helpers `0x3e940` and `0x3e390` (not just the first 65 instructions): follow every `swift_dynamicCast`, type descriptor/metadata reference, string literal and collector property write; derive a field map with evidence for each value. Cross-check with QA171 runtime type/key evidence when available. Then write a clean-room first-party observer/decoder and normalized `RoadAlertSample` + `ManeuverSample` transport with source identity, timestamps/expiry and no synthetic fallback; connect it to the shared DuoFace HUD across panes/apps/dashboard. Do not create another diagnostics-only release as a substitute for this decoder.
- **Current execution status:** this checkpoint only corrected the investigation record. No DuoFace source code changed, no new build/test/device run occurred, no Sileo publication or installation occurred. QA171 remains privately archived at run `41381981210`; its signed DEB hash and no-device-test flags remain as recorded above. Do not publish/install it without the existing package-conflict inventory and explicit release authorization.
